# SOLAR code deviations from Figma

Generated by `npm run solar:codegen` from `spec/tokens.json`, `spec/icons.json` and `spec/components/`. Foundations Figma file version `2026-09-20`; the `icon.*` and `logo.*` rows come from the SOLAR Icons file and the `component.*` rows from SOLAR Web, whose versions are recorded in their spec files.

Each row is a place where the generated code deliberately differs from what the Figma data says.
The mirror in `docs/` is never edited to accommodate these; take them to SOLAR governance.

| Token                                                                                      | Figma value                                                                                                                                                              | Why we differ                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Action                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `color.border.inverse`                                                                     | border/inverse                                                                                                                                                           | color.border.inverse is used in Figma both as a standalone value and as the namespace for finer variants (e.g. color.border.inverse.subtle). A DTCG tree node cannot cleanly be both, so it is emitted with its own $value and the variants nested beneath it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Ask SOLAR to give color.border.inverse an explicit base/default sibling name in Figma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `component.alert small.content.paddingLeft#unbound`                                        | 0, bound to no variable                                                                                                                                                  | paddingLeft on /content is a literal in Figma, bound to no variable, so the recipe carries a raw value where it should name a token.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | **Decided (bind, shared default zero-insets):** Same value, governed token: a padding Figma leaves unbound at 0 is inset.none, SOLAR's token for no inset. It is the one answer in every component, so it is decided once here and raised with SOLAR once, as a whole, not per component. Ask SOLAR to bind Alert Small /content paddingLeft to inset.none, which has the same value.                                                                                                              |
| `component.alert small.content.paddingRight#unbound`                                       | 0, bound to no variable                                                                                                                                                  | paddingRight on /content is a literal in Figma, bound to no variable, so the recipe carries a raw value where it should name a token.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | **Decided (bind, shared default zero-insets):** Same value, governed token: a padding Figma leaves unbound at 0 is inset.none, SOLAR's token for no inset. It is the one answer in every component, so it is decided once here and raised with SOLAR once, as a whole, not per component. Ask SOLAR to bind Alert Small /content paddingRight to inset.none, which has the same value.                                                                                                             |
| `component.alert small.root.width#unbound`                                                 | 466, bound to no variable                                                                                                                                                | width on / is a literal in Figma, bound to no variable, so the recipe carries a raw value where it should name a token.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | **Decided (set):** A callout spans the space it is put in: Figma draws it 466 wide, a sample. The caller sizes it. SOLAR has no control width token; ask SOLAR for one, or confirm 466 is intended.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `component.alert small.statusIndicator.height#unbound`                                     | 16, bound to no variable                                                                                                                                                 | height on /StatusIndicator is a literal in Figma, bound to no variable, so the recipe carries a raw value where it should name a token.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | **Decided (set):** As its width, the StatusIndicator's own. Ask SOLAR to bind Alert Small /StatusIndicator height to icon.sm, which has the same value.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `logo.os-logo.teams`                                                                       | 12 gradient fills and 7 fill-opacity attributes                                                                                                                          | The Teams mark is drawn with radial and linear gradients and per-path opacity, neither of which the vector IR represents. It ships as its raw SVG source instead of as paths. React and the raw SVG output render it faithfully -- React from JSX generated out of that source, with per-instance gradient ids -- but Flutter omits the Teams variant entirely: redrawing 11 radial gradients with focal points, gradientTransform matrices and 27 stops in a hand-written painter is disproportionate for one third-party mark, and approximating it with flat colours would invent a brand colour, which is worse than a missing asset. It is therefore the only asset the two targets do not share, and the parity suite asserts that it is the only one. | Ask SOLAR whether a flat-colour Teams mark exists.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
